language_arpetan = {
	type = language
	is_shown = { language_is_shown_trigger = { LANGUAGE = language_arpetan } }
	parameters = {
		language_subgroup_gallo_romance = yes
		language_group_romance = yes
		language_subbranch_italic = yes
		language_branch_italo_germanic = yes
		language_subfamily_centum = yes
		language_family_indo_european = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		value = 10
		if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_pillar = language_arpetan }
			multiply = same_language_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subgroup_gallo_romance }
			multiply = same_language_subgroup_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_group_romance }
			multiply = same_language_group_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subbranch_italic }
			multiply = same_language_subbranch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_branch_italo_germanic }
			multiply = same_language_branch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subfamily_centum }
			multiply = same_language_subfamily_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_family_indo_european }
			multiply = same_language_family_choice_factor
		}
	}

	color = arpitan
}

language_sogdian = {
	type = language
	is_shown = { language_is_shown_trigger = { LANGUAGE = language_sogdian } }
	parameters = {
		language_subgroup_northeastern_iranian = yes
		language_group_eastern_iranian = yes
		language_subbranch_iranic = yes
		language_branch_indo_iranian = yes
		language_subfamily_satem = yes
		language_family_indo_european = yes
		language_union_steppe_siberian = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		value = 10
		if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_pillar = language_sogdian }
			multiply = same_language_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subgroup_northeastern_iranian }
			multiply = same_language_subgroup_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_group_eastern_iranian }
			multiply = same_language_group_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subbranch_iranic }
			multiply = same_language_subbranch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_branch_indo_iranian }
			multiply = same_language_branch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subfamily_satem }
			multiply = same_language_subfamily_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_family_indo_european }
			multiply = same_language_family_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_union_steppe_siberian }
			multiply = same_language_union_choice_factor
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:character = {
					OR = {
						religion = religion:zoroastrianism_religion
						culture = { is_from_this_culture = { CULTURE = sogdian } }
					}
				}
			}
			multiply = same_language_union_choice_factor
		}
	}

	color = sogdian
}

			multiply = same_language_branch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subfamily_satem }
			multiply = same_language_subfamily_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_family_indo_european }
			multiply = same_language_family_choice_factor
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:character = {
					OR = {
						religion = religion:islam_religion
						religion = religion:zoroastrianism_religion
					}
				}
			}
			multiply = same_language_union_choice_factor
		}
	}

	color = kurdish
}

		}
	}

	color = turkmen

	is_shown = { language_is_shown_trigger = { LANGUAGE = language_arghu } }
	parameters = {
		dialect_continua_arghu_turk = yes
		language_group_shaz_turkic = yes
		language_branch_turkic = yes
		language_family_altaic = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		value = 10
		if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_pillar = language_arghu }
			multiply = same_language_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = dialect_continua_arghu_turk }
			multiply = same_language_dialect_continuum_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_group_shaz_turkic }
			multiply = same_language_group_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_branch_turkic }
			multiply = same_language_branch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_family_altaic }
			multiply = same_language_family_choice_factor
		}
	}

	color = khalaj
}

		language_union_british = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		value = 10
		if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_pillar = language_goidelic }
			multiply = same_language_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subgroup_goidelic }
			multiply = same_language_subgroup_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_group_insular_celtic }
			multiply = same_language_group_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subbranch_celtic }
			multiply = same_language_subbranch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_branch_italo_germanic }
			multiply = same_language_branch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subfamily_centum }
			multiply = same_language_subfamily_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_family_indo_european }
			multiply = same_language_family_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_union_british }
			multiply = same_language_union_choice_factor
		}
	}

	color = gaelic
}

		language_union_iberian = yes
	}

		value = 10

			multiply = same_language_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subgroup_iberian_romance }
			multiply = same_language_subgroup_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_group_romance }
			multiply = same_language_group_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subbranch_italic }
			multiply = same_language_subbranch_choice_factor

		}
		else_if = {

			multiply = same_language_union_choice_factor
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_pillar = language_basque } # Basque nobility spoke a much more latin-ised version of Basque
			multiply = same_language_union_choice_factor
		}
	}

	color = iberian

		language_branch_italo_germanic = yes
		language_subfamily_centum = yes
		language_family_indo_european = yes
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		value = 10
		if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_pillar = language_african_romance }
			multiply = same_language_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_group_romance }
			multiply = same_language_group_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subbranch_italic }
			multiply = same_language_subbranch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_branch_italo_germanic }
			multiply = same_language_branch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subfamily_centum }
			multiply = same_language_subfamily_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_family_indo_european }
			multiply = same_language_family_choice_factor
		}
	}

	color = african_roman
}

			multiply = same_language_subbranch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_branch_graeco_phrygian }
			multiply = same_language_branch_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_subfamily_centum }
			multiply = same_language_subfamily_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_family_indo_european }
			multiply = same_language_family_choice_factor
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_parameter = language_union_balkan }
			multiply = same_language_union_choice_factor
		}
	}

	color = phrygian
}

	type = language
	is_shown = { language_is_shown_trigger = { LANGUAGE = language_luwian } }
	parameters = {
		language_group_luwian = yes

		language_subfamily_anatolian = yes

		language_union_caucasus = yes
